Title: Llyfr Gweddi Gyffredin (the Book of Common Prayer in Welsh; host title 'The book of common prayer')
Creator: The Church of England in Wales
Publisher: Imprint not read in the OCR; the host record gives none
Publication Date: 1664 (host date; the preface refers to Charles II)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1664); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: cym
Source: archive.org item bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_the-book-of-common-pray_1664, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-10 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (645,094) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1664, out of US copyright by date. SCAN QUALITY: second witness only; quote only after checking the passage against the scanned page. Poor OCR: long s and noise at the head. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The Welsh Prayer Book after the Restoration: the Act of Uniformity at about OCR line 96; prayers for divers occasions at about 127; prayers at sea at about 153; the ordinal, 'Y Drefn ar modd i Ordeinio Esgobion, Offeiriaid a Diaconiaid', at about 155.

Profidier hefyd, ac ordeinier, ac enacter gan yr awdurdod ragddywedi- 
dic, fod i Faior Llundain, ac i Faiorau eraill. Ballad a phenſwyddogion 
eraill, o bawb oll or Hinaſoedd, Borwcheu, a Threfi corphoredic — 
y deyrnas hon, Cymru, a marſoedd yr un-rhyw , i'r rhai nid yw Juſtufiaid o

yr Act hon, i ymofyn, i wrando a therfynu y troſeddau a ddywedpwyd u- 
chod, a phob un o honynt bob blwyddyn o tewn pymtheng-nhiwrnod wedi 
Gwyl y Paſc, a San Michael Archangel, yn y cyfryw todd, a ffurf ag y 
mae yr Juſtuſiaid o Aſſiſ, ac Oyer a Determiner yn gallu gwneuthue.
